\nabla f
is Lipschitz continuous, then
\nabla f
is almost everywhere differentiable. Therefore, as the probability of hitting a null set is 0, we can infer that the potential function’s second derivative is sufficiently well defined to be applied in our setting.

\displaystyle\nabla f\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(\widetilde{Y}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}f\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+r(\widetilde{Y}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}-\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n})\big)dr}_{=:\,\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}}\,\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}-\widetilde{Y}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big[(X_{n}-Y_{n})+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})(V_{n}-U_{n})\big],

where
\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
is a symmetric positive semidefinite matrix which, from the assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), additionally satisfies
mI_{d}\preceq\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\preceq M_{1}I_{d}
.

Then the next stage in the mappings yields following differences,

\displaystyle\widetilde{V}^{(2)}_{n}-\widetilde{U}^{(2)}_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=e^{-\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}}(V_{n}-U_{n})-\frac{1}{2}e^{-\frac{1}{3}\gamma h_{n}}uh_{n}\Big(\nabla f\big(\widetilde{X}^{(1)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(\widetilde{Y}^{(1)}_{n}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg](X_{n}-Y_{n})+\bigg[\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\big(\frac{1}{3}\big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg](V_{n}-U_{n}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{X}^{(2)}_{n}-\widetilde{Y}^{(2)}_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=(X_{n}-Y_{n})+\frac{1-e^{-\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}}}{\gamma}\hskip 0.7113pt(V_{n}-U_{n})-\frac{1-e^{-\frac{1}{3}\gamma h_{n}}}{\gamma}uh_{n}\Big(\nabla f\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(\widetilde{Y}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\bigg[I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg](X_{n}-Y_{n})+\bigg[\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg](V_{n}-U_{n}).

From the definitions of
W_{n}
and
Z_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we can write

\displaystyle X_{n}-Y_{n}=\frac{Z_{n}-W_{n}}{z-w}\hskip 0.7113pt,\quad\quad V_{n}-U_{n}=\frac{zW_{n}-wZ_{n}}{z-w}\hskip 0.7113pt.

Thus,
W^{(1)}_{n}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle W^{(1)}_{n}

\displaystyle=w\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}-\widetilde{Y}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)+\big(\widetilde{V}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}-\widetilde{U}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)

\displaystyle=\bigg(w\bigg[I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg]+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg]\bigg)(X_{n}-Y_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+w\left[\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\right]\left(V_{n}-U_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg](V_{n}-U_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{z-w}\bigg(\bigg(w\bigg[I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg]+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg]\bigg)(Z_{n}-W_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+w\bigg[\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg](zW_{n}-wZ_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\bigg[\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\big(\frac{1}{3}\big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg](zW_{n}-wZ_{n})\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{z-w}\Big[\big(-wA-B+wzC+zD\big)W_{n}+\big(wA+B-w^{2}C-wD\big)Z_{n}\Big],

and similarly

\displaystyle Z^{(1)}_{n}=\frac{1}{z-w}\Big[\big(-zA-B+z^{2}C+zD\big)W_{n}+\big(zA+B-wzC-wD\big)Z_{n}\Big],

where

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n},\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\;\;\;\;B=-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle=\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n},\quad\quad D=\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})I_{d}-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\phi_{0}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt.

Note the matrices
A,B,C,D
are not only symmetric but also commute with one another. By expanding
\big\|W^{(1)}_{n}\big\|_{2}^{2}
and
\big\|Z^{(1)}_{n}\big\|_{2}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we obtain

\displaystyle\big\|W^{(1)}_{n}\big\|^{2}_{2}+\big\|Z^{(1)}_{n}\big\|^{2}_{2}=\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}}\big(W_{n}^{\top}EW_{n}+Z_{n}^{\top}FZ_{n}+2W_{n}^{\top}GZ_{n}\big),

(48)

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}E&=(w^{2}+z^{2})A^{2}+2B^{2}+z^{2}(w^{2}+z^{2})C^{2}+2z^{2}D^{2}+2\gamma AB-2z(w^{2}+z^{2})AC\\
&\hskip 21.33955pt-2\gamma zAD-2\gamma zBC-4zBD+2\gamma z^{2}CD,\\[3.0pt]
F&=(w^{2}+z^{2})A^{2}+2B^{2}+w^{2}(w^{2}+z^{2})C^{2}+2w^{2}D^{2}+2\gamma AB-2w(w^{2}+z^{2})AC\\
&\hskip 21.33955pt-2w\gamma AD-2\gamma wBC-4wBD+2\gamma w^{2}CD,\\[3.0pt]
G&=-(w^{2}+z^{2})A^{2}-2B^{2}-zw(w^{2}+z^{2})C^{2}-2zwD^{2}-2\gamma AB+\gamma(w^{2}+z^{2})AC\\
&\hskip 21.33955pt+\gamma^{2}AD+\gamma^{2}BC+2\gamma BD-2\gamma wzCD.\end{split}

Whilst we could bound the terms involving
A
,
B
,
C
and
D
by applying the inequality (
36
), since
-\phi_{i}(x)\leq|\phi_{i}(x)|
for all
x\in\big\{\lambda_{+}\hskip 0.7113pt,\lambda_{-}\hskip 0.7113pt,\frac{1}{3}\big\}
and
i\in\{0,1\}
this would produce too high an upper bound in order to prove the desired result. Instead, we will use the bounds below,

\displaystyle-\phi_{0}(x)\leq-1+x\gamma h_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\quad\quad-\phi_{1}(x)\leq-xh_{n}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma x^{2}h^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt.

In the following, we will also use

\displaystyle-h^{c}_{n}\sum^{m}_{i=1}\phi_{a_{i}}(b_{i})\leq 0,

for terms with
c+\sum^{m}_{i=1}a_{i}\geq 2
as they do not significantly impact the contraction rate
\alpha_{1}
and all the
\phi
functions are bounded below by
0
. Using the Loewner order
\preceq
and the commutativity of the matrices
A,B,C
and
D
, we can bound the matrices
E,F
and
G
as

\displaystyle E

\displaystyle\preceq(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(I_{d}+\frac{1}{9}u^{2}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+2\bigg(\frac{1}{4}u^{2}h^{2}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)+z^{2}(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(\lambda^{2}_{+}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{2}{9}\lambda^{2}_{-}u^{2}h^{6}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2z^{2}\bigg(I_{d}-2\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}I_{d}+2\lambda^{2}_{+}\gamma^{2}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{4}\lambda^{2}_{-}u^{2}_{n}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\gamma\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma uh^{2}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+\frac{1}{6}u^{2}h^{3}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2z(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(\lambda_{+}h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma\lambda^{2}_{+}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{3}uh^{3}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\gamma z\bigg(I_{d}-\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{2}_{+}\gamma^{2}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda_{-}u^{2}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\gamma z\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{+}uh^{2}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)+4z\bigg(\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)+2\gamma z^{2}\bigg(\lambda_{+}h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda^{2}_{-}u^{2}h^{5}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\preceq\Big((z-w)^{2}+(z-w)h_{n}\big(uM_{1}-2\lambda_{+}z^{2}\big)+h^{2}_{n}\Big(\gamma z+3\gamma^{2}z^{2}+z^{2}w^{2}+z^{4}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+h^{2}_{n}\big[(z+\gamma w+2\gamma z)uM_{1}+\big(1+\gamma+z^{2}+w^{2}+z^{2}w^{2}+z^{4}\big)u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt\big]\Big)I_{d}

\displaystyle=\Big((z-w)^{2}+(z-w)h_{n}\big(uM_{1}-2\lambda_{+}z^{2}\big)+E_{+}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big)I_{d}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle F

\displaystyle\preceq(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(I_{d}+\frac{1}{9}u^{2}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)+2\bigg(\frac{1}{4}u^{2}h^{2}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+w^{2}(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(\lambda^{2}_{+}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{2}{9}\lambda^{2}_{-}u^{2}h^{6}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2w^{2}\bigg(I_{d}-2\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}I_{d}+2\lambda^{2}_{+}\gamma^{2}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{4}\lambda^{2}_{-}u^{2}_{n}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\gamma\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma uh^{2}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+\frac{1}{6}u^{2}h^{3}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2w(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(\lambda_{+}h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma\lambda^{2}_{+}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{3}uh^{3}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\gamma w\bigg(I_{d}-\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{2}_{+}\gamma^{2}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda_{-}u^{2}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\gamma w\bigg(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{+}uh^{2}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)+4w\bigg(\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)+2\gamma w^{2}\bigg(\lambda_{+}h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda^{2}_{-}u^{2}h^{5}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\preceq\Big((z-w)^{2}+(z-w)h_{n}\big(2\lambda_{+}w^{2}-um\big)+h^{2}_{n}\big(\gamma w+3\gamma^{2}w^{2}+z^{2}w^{2}+w^{4}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+h_{n}^{2}\big[\big(w+\gamma z+2\gamma w\big)uM_{1}+\big(1+\gamma+z^{2}+w^{2}+z^{2}w^{2}+w^{4}\big)u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt\big]\Big)I_{d}

\displaystyle=\Big((z-w)^{2}+(z-w)h_{n}\big(2\lambda_{+}w^{2}-um\big)+F_{+}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big)I_{d}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle G

\displaystyle\preceq(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(-I_{d}+\frac{2}{3}uh^{2}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+zw(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(\lambda^{2}_{+}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}-\gamma\lambda^{3}_{+}h^{3}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{4}\gamma^{2}\lambda^{4}_{-}h^{4}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{2}{3}\lambda_{+}\lambda_{-}uh^{4}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2zw\Big(-I_{d}+2\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}I_{d}+\lambda_{-}uh^{2}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\gamma uh_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+\gamma(w^{2}+z^{2})\bigg(\lambda_{+}h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{9}\lambda_{-}u^{2}h^{5}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\gamma^{2}\bigg(I_{d}-\lambda_{+}\gamma h_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda^{2}_{+}\gamma^{2}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{6}\lambda_{-}u^{2}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\gamma^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{6}\lambda_{-}u^{2}h^{4}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}\bigg)+\gamma\bigg(-uh_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+\bigg(\frac{1}{3}+\lambda_{+}\bigg)\gamma uh^{2}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\gamma wz\bigg(-\lambda_{+}h_{n}I_{d}+2\gamma\lambda^{2}_{+}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{+}\lambda_{-}uh^{3}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}+\frac{1}{3}\lambda_{-}uh^{3}_{n}\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{-}\gamma u^{2}h^{3}_{n}\big(\mathcal{H}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\preceq h^{2}_{n}\Big[z^{3}w+zw^{3}+3\gamma^{2}zw+\gamma^{2}z^{3}w+\gamma^{2}zw^{3}+\gamma^{4}+\big(zw+z^{2}+w^{2}+2\gamma^{2}\big)uM_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\big(\gamma zw+z^{3}w+zw^{3}\big)uM_{1}+\big(1+\gamma+\gamma^{2}+\gamma z^{2}+\gamma w^{2}\big)u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\Big]I_{d}

\displaystyle=G_{+}h^{2}_{n}I_{d}\hskip 0.7113pt,

where we have used the assumption that
h_{n}\leq 1
and also bounded
\lambda_{+}
and
\lambda_{-}
for readability. Next, we will establish an
O(h_{n}^{2})
upper bound for
W_{n}^{\top}GZ_{n}
in terms of
\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}
and
\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

\displaystyle 4\hskip 0.7113ptW_{n}^{\top}GZ_{n}

\displaystyle=(W_{n}+Z_{n})^{\top}G(W_{n}+Z_{n})-(W_{n}-Z_{n})^{\top}G(W_{n}-Z_{n})

\displaystyle\leq\big|(W_{n}+Z_{n})^{\top}G(W_{n}+Z_{n})\big|+\big|(W_{n}-Z_{n})^{\top}G(W_{n}-Z_{n})\big|

\displaystyle\leq G_{+}h^{2}_{n}\big\|W_{n}+Z_{n}\big\|_{2}^{2}+G_{+}h^{2}_{n}\big\|W_{n}-Z_{n}\big\|_{2}^{2}\,,

which, by the triangle and Young’s inequalities, implies that

\displaystyle W_{n}^{\top}GZ_{n}

\displaystyle\leq G_{+}h^{2}_{n}\Big(\big\|W_{n}\big\|_{2}^{2}+\big\|Z_{n}\big\|_{2}^{2}\Big).

Therefore, putting this all together gives

\displaystyle\big\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big\|^{2}_{2}+\big\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=\frac{1}{(z-w)^{2}}\Big(W_{n}^{\top}EW_{n}+Z_{n}^{\top}FZ_{n}+2\hskip 0.7113ptW_{n}^{\top}GZ_{n}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{h_{n}}{z-w}\Big((uM_{1}-2\lambda_{+}z^{2})\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+(2\lambda_{+}w^{2}-um)\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 41.25648pt+\frac{h^{2}_{n}}{(z-w)^{2}}\Big(E_{+}\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+F_{+}\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+2\hskip 0.7113ptG_{+}\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{2}\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\bigg(1+\frac{h_{n}}{z-w}\max\big((uM_{1}-2\lambda_{+}z^{2}),(2\lambda_{+}w^{2}-um)\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 39.83385pt+\frac{h^{2}_{n}}{(z-w)^{2}}\big(\max(E_{+},F_{+})+2\hskip 0.7113ptG_{+}\big)\bigg)\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt=(1-\alpha_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n})\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{2}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{2}\big).

The result now follows by taking expectations of both sides.
∎

Similarly, we can show that the second map
\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)\to\big(\widetilde{X}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)
is also contractive.

Theorem 26
.

Consider the two approximations
(X,V)
and
(Y,U)
under the mappings
\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)\to\big(\widetilde{X}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)
and
\big(\widetilde{Y}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{U}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)\to\big(\widetilde{Y}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{U}_{n+1}\big)
that are driven by the same Brownian motion but are evolved from the initial conditions
\big(\widetilde{X}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)
and
\big(\widetilde{Y}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{U}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)
. Just as before, we define the following coordinate transformations,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\begin{pmatrix}W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\\[3.0pt]
Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\end{pmatrix}&=\begin{pmatrix}\big(wX^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}+V^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)-\big(wY^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}+U^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(zX^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}+V^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)-\big(zY^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}+U^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)\end{pmatrix},\\[5.0pt]
\begin{pmatrix}W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\\[3.0pt]
Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\end{pmatrix}&=\begin{pmatrix}\big(w\widetilde{X}_{n+1}+\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)-\big(w\widetilde{Y}_{n+1}+\widetilde{U}_{n+1}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(z\widetilde{X}_{n+1}+\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)-\big(z\widetilde{Y}_{n+1}+\widetilde{U}_{n+1}\big)\end{pmatrix},\end{split}

(49)

where
w\in\big[0,\frac{1}{2}\gamma\big)
and
z=\gamma-w
. Then, under assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), we have

\displaystyle\big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)\leq(1-\alpha_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n})\big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big),

(50)

for all
0<h_{n}\leq 1
and
n\geq 0
where

\displaystyle\alpha_{2}=\frac{(2\lambda_{-}z^{2}-uM_{1})\wedge(um-2\lambda_{-}w^{2})}{z-w}-K_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(51)

for some constant
K_{2}>0
.

Proof.

The proof strategy is identical to that of Theorem
25
. The main difference is simply the values of the constants
E_{+}
,
F_{+}
, and
G_{+}
. Specifically, these constants are given by

\displaystyle E_{+}

\displaystyle=\gamma^{2}z^{2}+\gamma z^{3}+\gamma zw^{2}+z^{2}w^{2}+z^{4}+\big(\gamma+\gamma z+zw^{2}+z^{3}\big)uM_{1}+(1+\gamma)u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle F_{+}

\displaystyle=\gamma^{2}w^{2}+\gamma w^{3}+\gamma z^{2}w+z^{2}w^{2}+w^{4}+\big(\gamma+\gamma w+z^{2}w+w^{3}\big)uM_{1}+(1+\gamma)u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle G_{+}

\displaystyle=\gamma^{2}zw+\gamma^{4}+(\gamma^{2}+z^{2}+w^{2})uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt.

∎

Next, we will show that the third mapping
\big(\widetilde{X}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)\to(X_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n+1})
is always small.

Theorem 27
.

Consider the two approximations
(X,V)
and
(Y,U)
under the mappings
\big(\widetilde{X}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)\to\big(X_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n+1}\big)
and
\big(\widetilde{Y}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{U}_{n+1}\big)\to\left(Y_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,U_{n+1}\right)
that are driven by the same Brownian motion but evolved from initial conditions
\big(\widetilde{X}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)
and
\big(\widetilde{Y}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widetilde{U}_{n+1}\big)
. Just as before, we define the following coordinate transformations,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\begin{pmatrix}W_{n}\\[3.0pt]
Z_{n}\end{pmatrix}&=\begin{pmatrix}\big(wX_{n}+V_{n}\big)-\big(wY_{n}+U_{n}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(zX_{n}+V_{n}\big)-\big(zY_{n}+U_{n}\big)\end{pmatrix},\\[5.0pt]
\begin{pmatrix}W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\\[3.0pt]
Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\end{pmatrix}&=\begin{pmatrix}\big(w\overline{X}_{n}+\overline{V}_{n}\big)-\big(w\overline{Y}_{n}+\overline{U}_{n}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(z\overline{X}_{n}+\overline{V}_{n}\big)-\big(z\overline{Y}_{n}+\overline{U}_{n}\big)\end{pmatrix},\end{split}

(52)

where
w\in\left[0,\frac{1}{2}\gamma\right)
and
z=\gamma-w
. Then, under assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), we have

\displaystyle\big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)\leq K_{3}\,h^{4}_{n}\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big),

(53)

for all
0<h_{n}\leq 1
and
n\geq 0
where
K_{3}>0
is a constant not depending on
h_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Proof.

Again, the proof strategy is nearly identical to that of Theorem
25
. Here, we use

\displaystyle E\leq E_{+}\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\quad\quad\text{and}\quad\quad F\leq F_{+}\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

where

\displaystyle E_{+}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\gamma+z^{2}+w^{2}+\gamma z^{2}+z^{2}w^{2}+z^{4}\right)u^{2}M^{2}_{1},

\displaystyle F_{+}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\gamma+z^{2}+w^{2}+\gamma w^{2}+z^{2}w^{2}+w^{4}\right)u^{2}M^{2}_{1},

and

\displaystyle G_{+}

\displaystyle\leq(\gamma+\gamma^{2}+\gamma z^{2}+\gamma w^{2})u^{2}M^{2}_{1}h^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt.

∎

Finally, we are now in a position to prove our main contractivity result (Theorem
8
).

Theorem 28

(Contractivity of the QUICSORT method (
1
))
.

Let
(X,V)
and
(Y,U)
denote two numerical approximations defined by the mappings
(X_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n})\to\big(X_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n+1})
and
(Y_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,Y_{n})\to(Y_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,U_{n+1})
which are driven by the same Brownian motion but evolved from initial conditions
(X_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n})
and
(Y_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,U_{n})
. We define the coordinate transformations,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\begin{pmatrix}W_{n}\\[3.0pt]
Z_{n}\end{pmatrix}&=\begin{pmatrix}\big(wX_{n}+V_{n}\big)-\big(wY_{n}+U_{n}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(zX_{n}+V_{n}\big)-\big(zY_{n}+U_{n}\big)\end{pmatrix},\\[5.0pt]
\begin{pmatrix}W_{n+1}\\[3.0pt]
Z_{n+1}\end{pmatrix}&=\begin{pmatrix}\big(wX_{n+1}+V_{n+1}\big)-\big(wY_{n+1}+U_{n+1}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(zX_{n+1}+V_{n+1}\big)-\big(zY_{n+1}+U_{n+1}\big)\end{pmatrix},\end{split}

(54)

where
w\in\big[0,\frac{1}{2}\gamma\big)
and
z=\gamma-w
. Then, under the assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), we have

\big(\|W_{n+1}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z_{n+1}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)\leq(1-2\alpha h_{n})\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big),

(55)

for all
0<h_{n}\leq 1
and
n\geq 0
where

\alpha=\frac{1}{z-w}\min\Big[z^{2}-uM_{1},\,um-w^{2},\,(\lambda_{-}z^{2}-\lambda_{+}w^{2})-\frac{1}{2}u(M_{1}-m)\Big]-Kh_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(56)

for some constant
K>0
not depending on
h_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Proof.

From the decomposition (
44
) and coordinate transformations (
49
) and (
52
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}W_{n+1}\\[3.0pt]
Z_{n+1}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\big(w\widetilde{X}_{n+1}+\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)-\big(w\widetilde{Y}_{n+1}+\widetilde{U}_{n+1}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(z\widetilde{X}_{n+1}+\widetilde{V}_{n+1}\big)-\big(z\widetilde{Y}_{n+1}+\widetilde{U}_{n+1}\big)\end{pmatrix}+\begin{pmatrix}\big(w\overline{X}_{n}+\overline{V}_{n}\big)-\big(w\overline{Y}_{n}+\overline{U}_{n}\big)\\[3.0pt]
\big(z\overline{X}_{n}+\overline{V}_{n}\big)-\big(z\overline{Y}_{n}+\overline{U}_{n}\big)\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\\[3.0pt]
Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\end{pmatrix}+\begin{pmatrix}W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\\[3.0pt]
Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\end{pmatrix}.

By applying the bounds (
46
), (
50
) and (
53
) given by the previous theorems along with the inequality (
43
) in Theorem
24
with
c=h^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we obtain

\displaystyle\big(\|W_{n+1}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z_{n+1}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)+\big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)+2\hskip 0.7113pt\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\langle W_{n}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)},W_{n}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}\big\rangle+\big\langle Z_{n}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)},Z_{n}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}\big\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq(1+h^{2}_{n})\big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)+\bigg(1+\frac{1}{h^{2}_{n}}\bigg)\Big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq(1+h^{2}_{n})(1-\alpha_{2}h_{n})\big(\|W^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)+(h^{2}_{n}+h^{4}_{n})K_{3}\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\big((1+h^{2}_{n})(1-\alpha_{1}h_{n})(1-\alpha_{2}h_{n})+(h^{2}_{n}+h^{4}_{n})K_{3}\big)\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq(1-2\alpha h_{n})\big(\|W_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\|Z_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big),

where

\displaystyle\alpha

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\lambda_{+}z^{2}-\frac{1}{2}uM_{1}\big)\wedge\big(\frac{1}{2}um-\lambda_{+}w^{2}\big)}{z-w}+\frac{\big(\lambda_{-}z^{2}-\frac{1}{2}uM_{1}\big)\wedge\big(\frac{1}{2}um-\lambda_{-}w^{2}\big)}{z-w}-Kh_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{z-w}\min\Big[z^{2}-uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\,\lambda_{+}z^{2}-\lambda_{-}w^{2}-\frac{1}{2}uM_{1}+\frac{1}{2}um,

\displaystyle\hskip 91.04881pt\lambda_{-}z^{2}-\lambda_{+}w^{2}-\frac{1}{2}uM_{1}+\frac{1}{2}um,\,um-w^{2}\Big]-Kh_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{z-w}\min\Big[z^{2}-uM_{1},\,um-w^{2},\,(\lambda_{-}z^{2}-\lambda_{+}w^{2})-\frac{1}{2}u(M_{1}-m)\Big]-Kh_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

and

\displaystyle K=1+\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{1}\cdot\alpha_{2}+K_{1}+K_{2}+K_{3}\hskip 0.7113pt.

∎

Remark 29
.

Provided that
\gamma^{2}>\frac{3+\sqrt{3}}{2}uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
, the contraction rate
\alpha
becomes positive if
w=0
and
h_{n}
is sufficiently small.

Remark 30
.

We can recover the result from Theorem
8
using the inequality
1-x\leq e^{-x}
. Although it is not strictly necessary for the proof of the global error, it does establish how the QUICSORT method undergoes exponential contractivity at each time interval
[t_{n},t_{n+1}]
.

Appendix C
Local error bounds

In this section we prove the local error bounds in Theorem
10
. To do so, we compare the Taylor expansions of underdamped Langevin dynamics (
2
) and the QUICSORT scheme (
1
).

Theorem 31

(Stochastic Taylor expansion of Langevin dynamics)
.

Let
\{x_{t},v_{t}\}_{t\hskip 0.7113pt\geq\hskip 0.7113pt0}
be the underdamped Langevin diffusion (
2
). Then, for all
n\geq 0
, over any interval
[t_{n},t_{n+1}]
,

\displaystyle x_{t_{n+1}}=x_{t_{n}}

\displaystyle+h_{n}v_{t_{n}}+\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x},\hskip 142.26378pt

(57a)

\displaystyle=x_{t_{n}}

\displaystyle+h_{n}v_{t_{n}}+\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}-\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\big(-\gamma v_{t_{n}}-u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big)

\displaystyle-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x},

(57b)

\displaystyle=x_{t_{n}}

\displaystyle+h_{n}v_{t_{n}}+\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}+\frac{1}{2}h_{n}^{2}\big(-\gamma v_{t_{n}}-u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big)

\displaystyle-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+\frac{1}{6}h^{3}_{n}\Big(\gamma^{2}v_{t_{n}}+\gamma u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{t_{n}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{3}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}.

(57c)

\displaystyle v_{t_{n+1}}=v_{t_{n}}

\displaystyle+\sigma W_{n}+h_{n}\big(-\gamma v_{t_{n}}-u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big)-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v},

(58a)

\displaystyle=v_{t_{n}}

\displaystyle+\sigma W_{n}+h_{n}\big(-\gamma v_{t_{n}}-u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big)-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\Big(\gamma^{2}v_{t_{n}}+\gamma u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{t_{n}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v},

(58b)

\displaystyle=v_{t_{n}}

\displaystyle+\sigma W_{n}+h_{n}\big(-\gamma v_{t_{n}}-u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big)-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1},

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\Big(\gamma^{2}v_{t_{n}}+\gamma u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{t_{n}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+\frac{1}{6}h^{3}_{n}\big(-\gamma^{3}v_{t_{n}}-\gamma^{2}u\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{3}\Big(2\gamma u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{t_{n}}-u^{2}\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})-u\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{t_{n}},v_{t_{n}})\Big)

\displaystyle-\gamma\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-2u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{3}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle-\sigma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\big(v_{r_{2}},W_{t_{n},r_{3}}\big)\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle-\sigma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\big(v_{t_{n}},(r_{2}-t_{n})W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\big)\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v},

(58c)

where the remainder terms
R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\hskip 0.7113pt
,
R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}
and
R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}
for the position component are given by

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}

\displaystyle:=-\gamma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}-u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\nabla f(x_{r_{2}})\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 56.9055pt\hskip 0.7113pt

(59a)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}

\displaystyle:=\gamma^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{3}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\nabla f(x_{r_{3}})\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{3}})v_{r_{3}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(59b)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}

\displaystyle:=-\gamma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{4}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla f(x_{r_{4}})\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{4}})\hskip 0.7113ptv_{r_{4}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{4}})(v_{r_{4}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{3}})\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(59c)

and the remainder terms
R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\hskip 0.7113pt
,
R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}
and
R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}
for the momentum component are given by

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}

\displaystyle:=\gamma^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\nabla f(x_{r_{2}})\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\big(\nabla f(x_{r_{1}})-\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\,dr_{1},

(60a)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}

\displaystyle:=-\hskip 0.7113pt\gamma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{3}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla f(x_{r_{3}})\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{3}})v_{r_{3}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\big(\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{2}})v_{r_{2}}-\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{r_{2}}\big)\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(60b)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}

\displaystyle:=\gamma^{2}\big(\gamma^{2}-2u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{4}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\big(\gamma^{2}-2u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla f(x_{r_{4}})\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{4}})v_{r_{4}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{4}})(v_{r_{4}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{3}})\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{r_{4}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{2}})\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\big(\nabla f(x_{r_{4}})\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{2}}\big)\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\big(v_{r_{3}},(r_{2}-t_{n})v_{t_{n}}\big)\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\big(\nabla f(x_{r_{3}}),(r_{2}-t_{n})v_{t_{n}}\big)\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\big(\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{3}})(v_{r_{3}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{2}})-\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{r_{3}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{2}})\big)\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}.

(60c)

Proof.

For any
r\in[t_{n},t_{n+1}]
, we can rewrite the SDE (
2
) into integral form:

\displaystyle x_{r}

\displaystyle=x_{t_{n}}+\int^{r}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{1}}\,dr_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle v_{r}

\displaystyle=v_{t_{n}}-\gamma\int^{r}_{t_{n}}v_{r_{1}}dr_{1}-u\int^{r}_{t_{n}}\nabla f(x_{r_{1}})\,dr_{1}+\sigma W_{t_{n},r}\hskip 0.7113pt.

Assuming
\nabla f
is sufficiently differentiable, by the fundamental theorem of calculus, we have

\displaystyle\nabla f(x_{r_{i}})

\displaystyle=\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})+\int^{r_{i}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{i+1}})\,dx_{r_{i+1}}

\displaystyle=\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})+\int^{r_{i}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{i+1}})v_{r_{i+1}}\,dr_{i+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{i}})v_{r}

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{r}+\int^{r_{i}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{i+1}})(v_{r},dx_{t_{i+1}})

\displaystyle=\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{r}+\int^{r_{i}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{i+1}})(v_{r},v_{r_{i+1}})\,dr_{i+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

for
t_{n}\leq r_{4}\leq r_{3}\leq r_{2}\leq r_{1}\leq t_{n+1}
. The theorem now follows by repeatedly substituting the above identities into the integral form of the SDE and rearranging the resulting terms.
∎

Theorem 32

(ULD Remainder Bounds)
.

Let
\{(x_{t},v_{t})\}_{t\geq 0}
be the underdamped Langevin diffusion (
2
) where we assume that
(x_{0},v_{0})\sim\pi
, the stationary distribution of the process. Let
0<h_{n}\leq 1
be fixed and consider the remainder terms given by (
59a
) and (
60a
), defined over the interval
[t_{n},t_{n+1}]
. Under assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), we can bound their
\mathbb{L}_{2}
norms and absolute means as

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\,\,\,\,\,\,\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(61)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\big]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(62)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x},C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
and
M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Under the additional assumption (
A.3
), we can bound the terms (
59b
) and (
60b
) as

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\,\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\,\,\,\,\,\,\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}\,dh_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(63)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\big]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\,\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}\,dh_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(64)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x},C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}>0
are constants which depend on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
and
M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt
. Finally, under the additional assumption (
A.4
), the remainder terms (
59c
) and (
60c
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\,dh_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\,\,\,\,\,\,\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\,d^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(65)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\big]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\,dh_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\,d^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(66)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x},C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
and
M_{3}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Proof.

Suppose that
\gamma^{2}\geq 2uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
. Then for all
x,v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\big\|(\gamma^{2}-2u\nabla^{2}f(x))v\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq\big\|(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x))v\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq(\gamma^{2}-um)\|v\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}.

We can derive the local error bounds (
61
), (
63
) and (
65
) for the position variable using this inequality, Fubini’s theorem and the bounds (
15
), (
18
), (
37
) and (
40
) given in Appendix
A
.

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{2}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla f(x_{r_{2}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\big(\gamma\sqrt{ud}+u\sqrt{M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptd}\,\big)

\displaystyle=C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{3}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 35.56593pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla f(x_{r_{3}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 35.56593pt+u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\|\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{3}})v_{r_{3}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{6}h^{3}_{n}\Big(\gamma^{2}\sqrt{ud}+\gamma u\sqrt{M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptd}+uM_{1}\sqrt{ud}\,\Big)

\displaystyle=C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma(\gamma^{2}-um)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{4}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 35.56593pt+u(\gamma^{2}-um)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla f(x_{r_{4}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 35.56593pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{4}})v_{r_{4}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 35.56593pt+u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{4}})(v_{r_{4}},v_{r_{3}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{24}h_{n}^{4}\Big(\gamma(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{ud}+u(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptd}+\gamma uM_{1}\sqrt{ud}+\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptud\hskip 0.7113pt\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{24}\Big(\gamma(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{u}+u(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{M_{1}}+\gamma uM_{1}\sqrt{u}+\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptu\hskip 0.7113pt\Big)dh_{n}^{4}

\displaystyle=C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}dh_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt.

For the momentum variable, we can additionally use (
42
) and the bound

\displaystyle\|x_{t}-x_{t_{n}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2p}}\leq\int^{t}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{1}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2p}}\,dr_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt,

to show that

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{2}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla f(x_{r_{2}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla f(x_{r_{1}})-\nabla f(x_{t_{n}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\Big(\gamma^{2}\sqrt{ud}+\gamma u\sqrt{M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptd}+uM_{1}\sqrt{ud}\,\Big)

\displaystyle=C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma(\gamma^{2}-um)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{3}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u(\gamma^{2}-um)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla f(x_{r_{3}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{3}})v_{r_{3}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{2}})v_{r_{2}}-\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})v_{r_{2}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{3}\Big(\gamma(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{ud}+u(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptd}+\gamma uM_{1}\sqrt{ud}+\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptud\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{6}\Big(\gamma\left(\gamma^{2}-um\right)\sqrt{u}+u(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{M_{1}}+\gamma uM_{1}\sqrt{u}+\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptu\Big)dh_{n}^{3}

\displaystyle=C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}dh_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma^{2}(\gamma^{2}-um)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{4}}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{4}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u(\gamma^{2}-um)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{4}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla f(x_{r_{4}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u(\gamma^{2}-um)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{2}f(x_{r_{4}})v_{r_{4}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{4}})(v_{r_{4}},v_{r_{3}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{r_{4}},v_{r_{2}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{3}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\big(\nabla f(x_{r_{4}}),v_{r_{2}}\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{4}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{r_{3}},(r_{2}-t_{n})v_{t_{n}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+u^{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\big(\nabla f(x_{r_{3}}),(r_{2}-t_{n})v_{t_{n}}\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(x_{r_{3}})(v_{r_{3}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{2}})-\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{r_{3}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{r_{2}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{24}h^{4}_{n}\Big(\gamma^{2}(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{ud}+\gamma u(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptd}+u(\gamma^{2}-um)M_{1}\sqrt{ud}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+3\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113pt\gamma u^{2}M_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptd+2\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113ptu^{2.5}\sqrt{M_{1}}M_{2}d+\sqrt{15}\hskip 0.7113ptu^{1.5}M_{3}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{1.5}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{24}\Big(\gamma^{2}(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{u}+\gamma u^{1.5}(\gamma^{2}-um)\sqrt{M_{1}}+u(\gamma^{2}-um)M_{1}\sqrt{u}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+3\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113pt\gamma u^{2}M_{2}+2\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113ptu^{2.5}\sqrt{M_{1}}M_{2}+\sqrt{15}\hskip 0.7113ptu^{1.5}M_{3}\Big)d^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}

\displaystyle=C_{v}^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}d^{1.5}h_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt.

In order to prove the bounds (
62
), (
64
) and (
66
) for the mean errors, we simply note that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}[R]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\|R\|_{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\|R\|_{2}^{2}\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}=\big\|R\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\hskip 0.7113pt,

by Jensen’s inequality.
∎

Theorem 33

(Taylor expansion of the QUICSORT method)
.

Let
\{X_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n}\}_{n\geq 0}
denote the QUICSORT method (
1
). Then for all
n\geq 0
, we have the following Taylor expansions:

\displaystyle X_{n+1}=X_{n}

\displaystyle+h_{n}V_{n}+\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X},

(67a)

\displaystyle=X_{n}

\displaystyle+h_{n}V_{n}+\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}+\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\big[-\gamma V_{n}-u\nabla f(X_{n})\big]

\displaystyle-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X},

(67b)

\displaystyle=X_{n}

\displaystyle+h_{n}V_{n}+\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}+\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\big[-\gamma V_{n}-u\nabla f(X_{n})\big]

\displaystyle-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+\frac{1}{6}h^{3}_{n}\big[\gamma^{2}V_{n}+\gamma u\nabla f(X_{n})-u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})V_{n}\big]

\displaystyle+\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\big)\bigg[\frac{1}{24}h_{n}^{3}W_{n}+\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{3}H_{n}+\frac{1}{2}h_{n}^{3}K_{n}\bigg]+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X},

(67c)

\displaystyle V_{n+1}=V_{n}

\displaystyle+\sigma W_{n}+h_{n}\big[-\gamma V_{n}-u\nabla f(X_{n})\big]-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V},

(68a)

\displaystyle=V_{n}

\displaystyle+\sigma W_{n}+h_{n}\big[-\gamma V_{n}-u\nabla f(X_{n})\big]-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\big[\gamma^{2}V_{n}+\gamma u\nabla f(X_{n})-u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})V_{n}\big]

\displaystyle+\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V},

(68b)

\displaystyle=V_{n}

\displaystyle+\sigma W_{n}+h_{n}\big[-\gamma V_{n}-u\nabla f(X_{n})\big]-\gamma\sigma\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{1}}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}h^{2}_{n}\big[\gamma^{2}V_{n}+\gamma u\nabla f(X_{n})-u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})V_{n}\big]

\displaystyle+\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\big)\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}-\frac{1}{6}h^{3}_{n}\big[\gamma^{3}V_{n}+\gamma^{2}u\nabla f(X_{n})\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{6}h^{3}_{n}\big[2\gamma u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})V_{n}-u^{2}\nabla f(X_{n})\nabla f(X_{n})-u\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})(V_{n},V_{n})\big]

\displaystyle-\gamma\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-2u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\big)\bigg[\frac{1}{24}h^{3}_{n}W_{n}+\frac{1}{6}h^{3}_{n}H_{n}+\frac{1}{2}h^{3}_{n}K_{n}\bigg]

\displaystyle-\sigma u\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\bigg(V_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\bigg[\frac{1}{8}h^{3}_{n}W_{n}+\frac{1}{3}h^{3}_{n}H_{n}+\frac{1}{2}h^{3}_{n}K_{n}\bigg]\bigg)+R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V},

(68c)

where

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}

\displaystyle=(\phi_{1}(1)-h_{n})\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)+\bigg[\frac{1}{h_{n}}\phi_{2}(1)-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\bigg]\sigma(W_{n}-12K_{n})\hskip 28.45274pt

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\big(\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\big)\nabla f(X_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big[\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{1}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\widetilde{I}_{1}\Big],

(69a)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\phi_{1}(1)-\bigg(h_{n}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma h^{2}_{n}\bigg)\bigg]\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[\frac{1}{h_{n}}\phi_{2}(1)-\bigg(\frac{1}{2}h_{n}-\frac{1}{6}\gamma h^{2}_{n}\bigg)\bigg]\sigma(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\big(\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\big)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}^{2}\bigg)\bigg]u\nabla f(X_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big[\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{1}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\widetilde{I}_{1}\Big],

(69b)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\phi_{1}(1)-\bigg(h_{n}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma h_{n}^{2}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma^{2}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[\frac{1}{h_{n}}\phi_{2}(1)-\bigg(\frac{1}{2}h_{n}-\frac{1}{6}\gamma h_{n}^{2}+\frac{1}{24}\gamma^{2}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]\sigma(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\bigg(\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\bigg)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}^{2}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]u\nabla f(X_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-h_{n}\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})-\bigg(-\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\bigg[V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\big(\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\big)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{24}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sigma u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big[\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{2}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{2}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\widetilde{I}_{1}\Big],

(69c)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}

\displaystyle=\big(\phi_{0}(1)-(1-\gamma h_{n})\big)\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[\frac{1}{h_{n}}\phi_{1}(1)-\bigg(1-\frac{1}{2}\gamma h_{n}\bigg)\bigg]\sigma(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\big)-(-h_{n})\bigg]u\nabla f(X_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big[\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{1}+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\widetilde{I}_{1}\Big],

(69d)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\phi_{0}(1)-(1-\gamma h_{n}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma^{2}h^{2}_{n})\bigg]\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[\frac{1}{h_{n}}\phi_{1}(1)-\bigg(1-\frac{1}{2}\gamma h_{n}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma^{2}h^{2}_{n}\bigg)\bigg]\sigma(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\big)-\bigg(-h_{n}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma h^{2}\bigg)\bigg]u\nabla f(X_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\big)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}^{2}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\Big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\Big)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{2}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sigma u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big[\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{2}+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{2}+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\widetilde{I}_{1}\Big],

(69e)

\displaystyle R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\phi_{0}(1)-\bigg(1-\gamma h_{n}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma^{2}h_{n}^{2}-\frac{1}{6}\gamma^{3}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[\frac{1}{h_{n}}\phi_{1}(1)-\bigg(1-\frac{1}{2}\gamma h_{n}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma^{2}h_{n}^{2}-\frac{1}{24}\gamma^{3}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]\sigma(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\big)-\bigg(-h+\frac{1}{2}\gamma h^{2}-\frac{1}{6}\gamma^{2}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]u\nabla f(X_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}\left(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\right)-\left(-\frac{1}{2}h_{n}^{2}+\frac{1}{3}\gamma h_{n}^{3}\right)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\big)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{2}+\frac{1}{12}\gamma h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sigma u\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})(W_{n}-12K_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[\frac{1}{2}h_{n}^{2}\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)-\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{3}\bigg]u^{2}\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\nabla f(X_{n})

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{4}h_{n}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{+})\big)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{6}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times u\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\big(V^{\otimes 2}_{n}+2\sigma V_{n}\otimes(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{4}h_{n}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{+})\big)\bigg]\sigma^{2}u\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\big(H_{n}+6K_{n}\big)^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\big)-\bigg(-\frac{1}{8}h_{n}^{3}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sigma u\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\big(V_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,W_{n}-12K_{n}\big)\allowbreak

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\sigma^{2}u\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\big(H_{n}+6K_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,W_{n}-12K_{n}\big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg[-\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{1}{h_{n}}\big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi^{2}_{2}(\lambda_{-})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi^{2}_{2}(\lambda_{+})\big)\bigg]\sigma^{2}u\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})(W_{n}-12K_{n})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big[\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{3}+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{3}+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\widetilde{I}_{2}+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\widetilde{I}_{3}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2}u^{2}h_{n}^{2}\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\widetilde{I}^{(1)},

(69f)

for
\lambda_{\pm}=\frac{3\pm\sqrt{3}}{6}
, along with the functions
\phi_{0}\hskip 0.7113pt
,
\phi_{1}
and
\phi_{2}
defined in (
35
), and

\displaystyle\begin{split}I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{1}&:=\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}f\big(X_{n}+tL^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big)L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\,dt\\[3.0pt]
&=\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\nabla f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f(X_{n})\Big],\\[5.0pt]
I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{2}&:=\int^{1}_{0}(1-t)\nabla^{3}f\big(X_{n}+tL^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big)\big(L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big)^{\otimes 2}dt\\[3.0pt]
&=\int^{1}_{0}\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\nabla^{2}f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big)-\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\Big]L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}dt,\\[5.0pt]
I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{3}&:=\int^{1}_{0}(1-t)\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\nabla^{3}f\big(X_{n}+tL^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big)-\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\Big]\big(L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big)^{\otimes 2}dt,\\[5.0pt]
\widetilde{I}_{1}\,&:=\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{2}f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}+tP_{n}\big)P_{n}\hskip 0.7113ptdt\\[3.0pt]
&=\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\nabla f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}+P_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}\big)\Big],\\[5.0pt]
\widetilde{I}_{2}\,&:=\int^{1}_{0}(1-t)\nabla^{3}f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}+tP_{n}\big)(P_{n})^{\otimes 2}dt\\[3.0pt]
&=\int^{1}_{0}\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\nabla^{2}f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}+tP_{n}\big)-\nabla^{2}f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}\big)\Big]P_{n}dt,\\[5.0pt]
\widetilde{I}_{3}\,&:=\int^{1}_{0}\nabla^{3}f\big(X_{n}+tL^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}\big)\big(L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,P_{n}\big)dt\\[3.0pt]
&=\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\nabla^{2}f\big(X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}\big)-\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})\Big]P_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\end{split}

(70)

where

\displaystyle L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}

\displaystyle:=\phi_{1}(\lambda_{\pm})\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)+\phi_{2}(\lambda_{\pm})C_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle P_{n}

\displaystyle:=-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\nabla f\big(X^{(1)}_{n}\big)=-uh_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)\Big(\nabla f(X_{n})+I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}\Big).

Proof.

We can rewrite the QUICSORT method (
1
) as

\displaystyle X_{n+1}

\displaystyle=X_{n}+\phi_{1}(1)\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)+\phi_{2}(1)C_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big(\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\nabla f\big(X^{(1)}_{n}\big)+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\nabla f\big(X^{(2)}_{n}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle V_{n+1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{0}(1)\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)-\sigma(H_{n}-6K_{n})+\phi_{1}(1)C_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\nabla f\big(X^{(1)}_{n}\big)+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\nabla f\big(X^{(2)}_{n}\big)\Big),

where

\displaystyle X^{(1)}_{n}

\displaystyle=X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{n},\hskip 28.45274ptX^{(2)}_{n}=X_{n}+L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}+P_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt.

Using Taylor expansions with an integral remainder, we can derive the identities in (
70
),

\displaystyle\begin{split}\nabla f\big(X^{(1)}_{n}\big)=\nabla f(X_{n})&+I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}\\[1.0pt]
=\nabla f(X_{n})&+\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{n}+I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{2}\\[1.0pt]
=\nabla f(X_{n})&+\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{n}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\big(L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{n},L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{n}\big)+I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{3},\\[3.0pt]
\nabla f\big(X^{(2)}_{n}\big)=\nabla f(X_{n})&+I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{1}+\widetilde{I}_{1}\\
=\nabla f(X_{n})&+\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}+I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{2}+\widetilde{I}_{1}\\[1.0pt]
=\nabla f(X_{n})&+\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}+\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})P_{n}+\frac{1}{2}\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})\big(L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n},L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}\big)\\[1.0pt]
&+I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{3}+\widetilde{I}_{2}+\widetilde{I}_{3}\hskip 0.7113pt.\end{split}

The result now follows by substituting the expansions for
\nabla f\big(X^{(1)}_{n}\big)
and
\nabla f\big(X^{(2)}_{n}\big)
into the QUICSORT method, and Taylor expanding the
\phi
functions (
35
) up to the desired order.
∎

Theorem 34

(Remainder bounds for the QUICSORT method)
.

Let
\{(X_{n},V_{n})\}_{n\geq 0}
denote the QUICSORT method (
1
) and let
\{(x_{t},v_{t})\}_{t\geq 0}
denote the underdamped Langevin diffusion (
2
) where we assume that
(x_{0},v_{0})\sim\pi
, the stationary distribution of the process. Suppose that at time
t_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we have
(X_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n})=(x_{t_{n}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{t_{n}})
. Let
0<h_{n}\leq 1
be fixed, and consider the remainder terms (
69a
) and (
69d
) defined over the time interval
[t_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,t_{n+1}]
. Under assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
) we can bound their
\mathbb{L}_{2}
norms and absolute means as

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\,\,\,\,\,\,\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(71)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}\big]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{2}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(72)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X},C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
and
M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Under the additional assumption (
A.3
), we can bound (
69b
) and (

) as

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\,\,\,\,\,\,\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}dh_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(73)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}\big]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}dh_{n}^{3}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(74)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X},C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
and
M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt
. Finally, under the additional assumption (
A.4
), then the remainder terms (

) and (

) can be bounded by

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}dh_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\,\,\,\,\,\,\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}d^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(75)

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}dh_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}d^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}^{4}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(76)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X},C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
and

.

Proof.

) using the Cauchy-Schwarz and Minkowski inequalities along with assumptions (

), (

), (

) and (

\displaystyle\big\|I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt\big\|L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(\pm)}_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\|P_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2u^{2}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\Big|\phi^{2}_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\|\nabla f(X_{n})\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|V_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\sigma^{2}u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\Big|\phi^{2}_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\|H_{n}+6K_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\sigma^{2}u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big|\phi^{2}_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi^{2}_{2}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\|W_{n}-12K_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\sigma u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\Big|\phi^{2}_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\|V_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|H_{n}+6K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{I}_{3}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\big\|L^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|P_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\sigma uM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big|\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\Big|\big\|\nabla f(X_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|W_{n}-12K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}|\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|\|V_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\sigma^{2}uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}|\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|\|H_{n}+6K_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\sigma^{2}uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big|\frac{1}{h_{n}}\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\|W_{n}-12K_{n}\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+2\sigma uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\Big|\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\|V_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|H_{n}+6K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt+\sigma uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\bigg(\Big|\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})\Big|+\Big|\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})\Big|\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 21.33955pt\hskip 14.22636pt\times\Big[\|V_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|W_{n}-12K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}+\sigma\|H_{n}+6K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|W_{n}-12K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\Big]\hskip 0.7113pt.

We can derive the remainder bounds (
71
), (
73
) and (
75
) for the position variable using the above inequalities along with equations (
15
), (
18
) and (

). In addition, we will use Theorem
22
and Taylor expand the
\phi_{i}
functions using the Lagrange form of the remainder.

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\gamma h^{2}_{n}\|V_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\gamma\sigma h^{2}_{n}\|H_{n}+6K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma\sigma h^{2}_{n}\|W_{n}-12K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2}uh^{2}_{n}\big\|\nabla f(X_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big(|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\big|\|I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|\widetilde{I}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\frac{1}{2}\gamma\sqrt{u}+\frac{1}{2}u\sqrt{M_{1}}+\frac{2+\sqrt{3}+\sqrt{5}}{12}\gamma\sigma\sqrt{h_{n}}+\frac{1}{6}u^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{15+10\sqrt{3}+9\sqrt{5}}{360}\sigma uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth^{1.5}_{n}+\frac{3-\sqrt{3}}{36}u^{2}M^{1.5}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{2-\sqrt{3}}{36}u^{2.5}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3}_{n}+\frac{15-5\sqrt{3}+21\sqrt{5}-11\sqrt{15}}{2160}\sigma u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3.5}_{n}\bigg]\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[\gamma\sqrt{u}+\gamma\sigma+u\sqrt{M_{1}}+u^{1.5}M_{1}+\sigma uM_{1}+u^{2}M^{1.5}_{1}+u^{2.5}M^{2}_{1}+\sigma u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\Big]\sqrt{d}h^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{6}\gamma^{2}h^{3}_{n}\|V_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{6}\gamma^{2}\sigma h^{3}_{n}\|H_{n}+6K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{24}\gamma^{2}\sigma h^{3}_{n}\|W_{n}-12K_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{6}\gamma uh^{3}_{n}\big\|\nabla f(X_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big(|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})|\big\|I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|\widetilde{I}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{30+25\sqrt{3}+21\sqrt{5}}{3600}\gamma^{3}\sigma\sqrt{h_{n}}+\frac{7+6\sqrt{3}+5\sqrt{5}}{432}\gamma uM_{1}\sqrt{h_{n}}

\displaystyle\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{8}\gamma^{2}\sigma uh^{4}_{n}\big\|\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{7}{288}\sigma^{2}uh^{3}_{n}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})(W_{n}-12K_{n})^{\otimes 2}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{8}\sigma^{2}uh^{3}_{n}\big\|\nabla^{3}f(X_{n})(H_{n}+6K_{n},W_{n}-12K_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2}u^{2}h^{2}_{n}\Big|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\Big|\big\|\nabla^{2}f(X_{n})I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big[|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})|\big\|I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(-)}_{3}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|I^{\hskip 0.7113pt(+)}_{3}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\Big]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|\widetilde{I}_{2}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|\widetilde{I}_{3}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\frac{1}{24}\gamma^{4}\sqrt{u}+\frac{1}{24}\gamma^{3}u\sqrt{M_{1}}+\frac{1}{8}\gamma^{2}u^{1.5}M_{1}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{6}\gamma u^{2}M_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{45+46\sqrt{3}+21\sqrt{5}+16\sqrt{15}}{720}\sigma^{2}uM_{2}+\frac{3-\sqrt{3}}{36}u^{2.5}M_{1}+\frac{1}{36}u^{3}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{\sqrt{3}+1}{12}u^{2.5}\sqrt{M_{1}}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{15}}{24}u^{2.5}M_{3}+\frac{30+25\sqrt{3}+21\sqrt{5}}{3600}\gamma^{4}\sigma\sqrt{h_{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{7+6\sqrt{3}+5\sqrt{5}}{288}\gamma^{2}\sigma uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}+\frac{360+235\sqrt{3}+119\sqrt{5}}{2160}\gamma\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{5+5\sqrt{3}-2\sqrt{15}}{360}\sigma u^{2}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}+\frac{16\sqrt{3}+9\sqrt{5}+7\sqrt{15}}{144}\sigma u^{2}M_{3}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{30+15\sqrt{3}+6\sqrt{5}+5\sqrt{15}}{360}\sigma u^{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}+\frac{4-\sqrt{3}}{36}\gamma u^{2}M^{1.5}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{\sqrt{3}-1}{36}u^{3.5}M^{1.5}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{18}u^{3}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{160+125\sqrt{3}+70\sqrt{5}+71\sqrt{15}}{720}\sigma^{2}u^{1.5}M_{3}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{5\sqrt{3}-6\sqrt{5}+5\sqrt{15}}{1080}\sigma u^{3}M^{1.5}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{1.5}_{n}+\frac{10+5\sqrt{3}+3\sqrt{15}}{360}\sigma u^{2.5}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{1.5}_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{2\sqrt{3}-3}{216}u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}+\frac{30+14\sqrt{3}+9\sqrt{5}+4\sqrt{15}}{2160}\sigma^{2}u^{2}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{-5+5\sqrt{3}-11\sqrt{5}+7\sqrt{15}}{2160}\sigma u^{1.5}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2.5}_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{1125+835\sqrt{3}+549\sqrt{5}+371\sqrt{15}}{25920}\sigma^{3}uM_{3}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2.5}_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\frac{-30+21\sqrt{3}-6\sqrt{5}+5\sqrt{15}}{12960}\sigma^{2}u^{3}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}h^{3}_{n}\bigg]d^{1.5}h^{4}_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\gamma^{4}\sqrt{u}+\gamma^{4}\sigma+\gamma^{3}u\sqrt{M_{1}}+\gamma^{2}u^{1.5}M_{1}+\gamma^{2}\sigma uM_{1}+u^{2.5}M_{1}+\sigma u^{2}M_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u^{2}M_{2}+\gamma\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}+\sigma^{2}uM_{2}+u^{2.5}M_{3}+\sigma u^{2}M_{3}+\sigma^{2}u^{1.5}M_{3}+\sigma^{3}uM_{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\gamma u^{2}M^{1.5}_{1}+u^{2.5}\sqrt{M_{1}}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}+\sigma u^{2}\sqrt{M_{1}}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}+u^{3}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}+\sigma u^{2.5}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\sigma^{2}u^{2}M_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}+u^{3.5}M^{1.5}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}+\sigma u^{3}M^{1.5}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}+u^{2}M^{2}_{1}M_{2}+\sigma u^{1.5}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113ptM_{2}
