Similar to the ULD remainder bounds (Theorem
32
), we can prove (
72
), (
74
) and (
76
) using

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}[R]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\|R\|_{2}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\|R\|_{2}^{2}\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}=\big\|R\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\hskip 0.7113pt,

by Jensen’s inequality.
∎

We are now in a position to obtain local error bounds for QUICSORT (Theorem
10
).

Theorem 35

(Local error bounds for the QUICSORT method)
.

Let
\{(X_{n},V_{n})\}_{n\hskip 0.7113pt\geq\hskip 0.7113pt0}
denote the QUICSORT method (
1
) and
\{(x_{t},v_{t})\}_{t\hskip 0.7113pt\geq\hskip 0.7113pt0}
be the underdamped Langevin diffusion (
2
) with the same underlying Brownian motion. Suppose the assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
) hold and assume further that
(x_{0},v_{0})\sim\pi
, the unique stationary measure of the process. Suppose that at time
t_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we have
(X_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n})=(x_{t_{n}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{t_{n}})
and let
0<h_{n}\leq 1
be fixed. Then, for any
n\geq 0
, the local strong error for the QUICSORT method at time
t_{n+1}
has the bound

\displaystyle\big\|X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{s,\,x}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{s,\,v}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(77)

and the local weak error is bounded by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\,x}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n},\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\,v}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(78)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{s,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{s,\,v}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\,v}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
and
M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Under the additional assumption (
A.3
), the local strong error at time
t_{n+1}
has the bound

\displaystyle\big\|X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{s,\,x}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{s,\,v}\hskip 0.7113ptdh^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(79)

and the local weak error is bounded by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\,x}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\,v}\hskip 0.7113ptdh^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(80)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{s,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{s,\,v}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\,v}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
and
M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt
. Finally, under the additional assumption (
A.4
), the local strong error at time
t_{n+1}
has the bound

\displaystyle\big\|X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{s,\,x}\hskip 0.7113ptdh^{3.5}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{s,\,v}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3.5}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(81)

and the local weak error is bounded by

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\,x}\hskip 0.7113ptdh^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 28.45274pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\,v}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(82)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{s,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{s,\,v}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\,v}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
and
M_{3}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Proof.

We first note that the Taylor expansions of ULD and QUICSORT are identical for all terms that are
O(h^{3})
or larger. Then, under the assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), we have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}=\big\|X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{X}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{x}\Big]\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}=\big\|V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{V}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{v}\Big]\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

and, if we further assume (
A.3
), then

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}=\big\|X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{X}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{x}\Big]\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}=\big\|V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{V}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{v}\Big]d\hskip 0.7113pth^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt.

In the case where we additionally assume (
A.4
), we note that the triple time integral of the Brownian motion is centred, and we can write it in terms of
W_{n}
,
H_{n}
,
K_{n}
,
M_{n}
using
(
33
)
. Moreover, the other stochastic integrals are centred and we can bound the following terms,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\bigg\|\sigma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{r_{2}},W_{t_{n},r_{3}})\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sigma uM_{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\|v_{r_{2}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\|W_{t_{n},r_{3}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113pt\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptd\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}(r_{3}-t_{n})^{\frac{1}{2}}\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{8\sqrt{3}}{105}\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptd\hskip 0.7113pth^{3.5}_{n},\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sigma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})\left(v_{t_{n}},(r_{2}-t_{n})W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\right)\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sigma uM_{2}\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}(r_{2}-t_{n})\|v_{t_{n}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\big\|W_{t_{n},r_{2}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{4}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\sqrt{3}\hskip 0.7113pt\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptd\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}(r_{2}-t_{n})^{\frac{3}{2}}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\leq\frac{4\sqrt{3}}{35}\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptd\hskip 0.7113pth^{3.5}_{n},

by Fubini’s theorem along with the bounds (
18
), (
34
) and (
40
). We can show (
81
) and (
82
) for the position variable as follows

\displaystyle\big\|X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\bigg(\frac{1}{60}h^{3}_{n}H_{n}-h^{3}_{n}M_{n}\bigg)\bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\hskip-5.69054pt+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\frac{7\sqrt{3}+3\sqrt{7}}{2520}\sigma(\gamma^{2}-um)+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}\hskip 0.7113pt\bigg]d\hskip 0.7113pth^{3.5}_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[\hskip 0.7113pt\sigma(\gamma^{2}-um)+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big]d\hskip 0.7113pth^{3.5}_{n},

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{X}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{x}\Big]d\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

and for the momentum variable,

\displaystyle\big\|V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\sigma\big(\gamma^{2}-2u\nabla^{2}f(x_{t_{n}})\big)\bigg(\frac{1}{60}h^{3}_{n}H_{n}-h^{3}_{n}M_{n}\bigg)\bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg\|\sigma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{2}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{r_{2}},W_{t_{n},r_{3}})\,dr_{3}\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\bigg\|\sigma u\int^{t_{n+1}}_{t_{n}}\int^{r_{1}}_{t_{n}}\nabla^{3}f(x_{t_{n}})(v_{t_{n}},(r_{2}-t_{n})W_{t_{n},r_{2}})\,dr_{2}\,dr_{1}\bigg\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\frac{7\sqrt{3}+3\sqrt{7}}{2520}\sigma(\gamma^{2}-um)+\frac{12\sqrt{3}}{35}\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{h_{n}}\hskip 0.7113pt\bigg]d^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3.5}_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\Big[\sigma(\gamma^{2}-um)+\sigma u^{1.5}M_{2}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\Big]d^{1.5}h^{3.5},

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\big]\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|R^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq\Big[C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{V}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{v}\Big]d^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

where we have used Minkowski’s inequality and the bounds on the remainder terms.
∎

Corollary 36
.

Under the same settings as used in Theorem
35
, assuming (
A.1
) and (
A.2
), we have the additional bounds

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\hskip 0.7113ptx}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 14.22636pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\hskip 0.7113ptv}\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(83)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,\,v}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
and
M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Under the additional assumption (
A.3
), we have the bounds

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\hskip 0.7113ptx}\hskip 0.7113ptdh^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 14.22636pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\hskip 0.7113ptv}\hskip 0.7113ptd\hskip 0.7113pth^{3}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(84)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,\,v}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
and
M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Finally, under the additional assumption (
A.4
), we have the bounds

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[X_{n+1}-x_{t_{n+1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\hskip 0.7113ptx}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\hskip 9.3894pt\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[V_{n+1}-v_{t_{n+1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\hskip 0.7113ptv}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pt\hskip 0.7113pth^{4}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(85)

where
C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\,x}\hskip 0.7113pt,C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,\,v}\hskip 0.7113pt>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
and
M_{3}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Proof.

Notice that the Taylor expansion for the ULD and the QUICSORT method are identical up to order
h^{3}
, and the only mismatch are the stochastic integrals of order
h^{3.5}
. However, these integrals have a conditional mean of zero, and thus do not contribute to the above errors. Moreover, for both the position and momentum variables’ remainder terms, under any of the smoothness assumptions,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}=\Big(\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[R\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{2}^{2}\ \Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\Big(\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbb{E}\big[\,\|R\|_{2}^{2}\mid\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\ \Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}=\|R\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\hskip 0.7113pt,

by Jensen’s inequality and the tower property of conditional expectations. The result now follows by applying the same logic as used in the proof of Theorem
35
to establish the local weak error bounds.
∎

Remark 37
.

The bounds for the
\mathbb{L}_{2}
norm of the absolute conditional mean are identical to the bounds for the local weak error. This will allow us to use them interchangeably when proving the global error result (Theorem
7
).

Appendix D
Global error bounds

In this section, we prove the global error bounds for the QUICSORT method (Theorem
7
). To do so, we use the methodology outlined in Section
3
. We will also use the following theorem.

Theorem 38
.

Consider two approximations
Q=(X,V)
and
\widehat{Q}=(Y,U)
under the mapping
Q_{n}\to Q_{n+1}
and
\widehat{Q}_{n}\to\widehat{Q}_{n+1}
defined in equation (
1
), driven by the same Brownian motion but evolved from the initial conditions
Q_{n}=(X_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n})
and
\widehat{Q}_{n}=(Y_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,U_{n})
respectively. Suppose assumption (
A.2
) holds and let
0<h_{n}\leq 1
. Then for all
n\geq 0

\displaystyle\big\|(Q_{n+1}-Q_{n})-(\widehat{Q}_{n+1}-\widehat{Q}_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C_{q}\big\|Q_{n}-\widehat{Q}_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

(86)

where
C_{q}>0
is a constant depending on
\gamma
,
u
, and
M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Proof.

We first rewrite the QUICSORT method (
1
) as

\displaystyle X_{n+1}

\displaystyle=X_{n}+\phi_{1}(1)\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)+\phi_{2}(1)C_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big(\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\nabla f\big(X^{(1)}_{n}\big)+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\nabla f\big(X^{(2)}_{n}\big)\Big),

\displaystyle V_{n+1}

\displaystyle=\phi_{0}(1)\Big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)+\sigma(H_{n}-6K_{n})+\phi_{1}(1)C_{n}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}\Big(\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\nabla f(X^{(1)}_{n})+\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\nabla f\big(X^{(2)}_{n}\big)\Big),

where

\displaystyle X^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}

\displaystyle=X_{n}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)+\phi_{2}(\lambda_{-})C_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

\displaystyle X^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}

\displaystyle=X_{n}+\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\big(V_{n}+\sigma(H_{n}+6K_{n})\big)+\phi_{2}(\lambda_{+})C_{n}-h_{n}\phi_{1}\bigg(\frac{1}{3}\bigg)u\nabla f\big(X^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big).

Since the approximations
Q
and
\widehat{Q}
use the same Brownian motion, we can show that

\displaystyle\big\|(X_{n+1}-X_{n})-(Y_{n+1}-Y_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq|\phi_{1}(1)|\|V_{n}-U_{n}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})|\big\|\nabla f\big(X^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(Y^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 35.56593pt+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|\nabla f\big(X^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(Y^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{1}{2}uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\bigg(\hskip 0.7113pt|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})|+|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|+uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\Big|\hskip 0.7113pt\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\hskip 0.7113pt\Big|\hskip 0.7113pt\bigg)\big\|X_{n}-Y_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 35.56593pt+\bigg(|\phi_{1}(1)|+uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}|\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|+\frac{1}{2}u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\Big|\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi^{2}_{1}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\bigg)\big\|V_{n}-U_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\big(1+uM_{1}+u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\big)\big\|Q_{n}-\widehat{Q}_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}h_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

and, similarly for the momentum variable, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\big\|(V_{n+1}-V_{n})-(U_{n+1}-U_{n})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq|\phi_{0}(1)-1|\big\|V_{n}-U_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})|\big\|\nabla f\big(X^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(Y^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{n}\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\\
&\hskip 35.56593pt+\frac{1}{2}uh_{n}|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})|\big\|\nabla f\big(X^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)-\nabla f\big(Y^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{n}\big)\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\frac{1}{2}uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\bigg[\hskip 0.7113pt|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})|+|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})|+uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\Big|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\Big|\hskip 0.7113pt\bigg]\big\|X_{n}-Y_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\\
&\hskip 35.56593pt+\bigg(\hskip 0.7113pt|\phi_{0}(1)-1|+\frac{1}{2}uM_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth_{n}\big(|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{+})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})|+|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{1})\phi_{1}(\lambda_{+})|\big)\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt+\frac{1}{2}u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\hskip 0.7113pth^{2}_{n}\Big|\phi_{0}(\lambda_{-})\phi_{1}\Big(\frac{1}{3}\Big)\phi_{1}(\lambda_{-})\Big|\hskip 0.7113pt\bigg)\big\|V_{n}-U_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
&\hskip 14.22636pt\leq\big(\gamma+2uM_{1}+u^{2}M^{2}_{1}\big)\big\|Q_{n}-\widehat{Q}_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}h_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt.\end{split}

The result follows by summing the bounds for the position and momentum variables.
∎

Finally, we will show the main result of our paper (global error bounds for QUICSORT).

Theorem 39

(Global error bounds for the QUICSORT method)
.

Let
\{(X_{n},V_{n})\}_{n\hskip 0.7113pt\geq\hskip 0.7113pt0}
denote the QUICSORT method (
1
) and let
\{(x_{t},v_{t})\}_{t\hskip 0.7113pt\geq\hskip 0.7113pt0}
be the underdamped Langevin diffusion (
2
) driven by the same underlying Brownian motion. Suppose assumptions (
A.1
) and (
A.2
) hold. Further suppose that
(x_{0},v_{0})\sim\pi
, the unique stationary measure of the diffusion process and that the approximation and the SDE solution have the same initial velocity
V_{0}=v_{0}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,uI_{d})
. Let
0<h\leq 1
be fixed and
\gamma\geq 2\sqrt{uM_{1}}
. Then for all
n\geq 0
, the global error at time
t_{n}
has the bound

\displaystyle\|X_{n}-x_{t_{n}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq ce^{-n\beta h}\|X_{0}-x_{0}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+C_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt\sqrt{d}\hskip 0.7113pth,

where
c,\beta,C_{1}>0
are constants depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
and
M_{1}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Under the additional assumption (
A.3
), the global error at time
t_{n}
satisfies the bound

\displaystyle\|X_{n}-x_{t_{n}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq ce^{-n\beta h}\|X_{0}-x_{0}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+C_{2}\hskip 0.7113ptd\hskip 0.7113pth^{2},

where
C_{2}>0
is a constant depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
and
M_{2}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Finally, under the additional assumption (
A.4
), the global error at time
t_{n}
has the bound

\displaystyle\|X_{n}-x_{t_{n}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq ce^{-\frac{1}{2}n\beta h}\|X_{0}-x_{0}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+C_{3}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{1.5}\hskip 0.7113pth^{3},

where
C_{3}>0
is a constant depending on
\gamma
,
u
,
m
,
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
and
M_{3}\hskip 0.7113pt
.

Proof.

The approach for this proof will follow the strategy that was outlined in Section
3
.
We will write
Q_{n}=(X_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,V_{n})^{\top}
,
q_{t_{n}}=(x_{t_{n}}\hskip 0.7113pt,v_{t_{n}})^{\top}
and define the coordinate transformation

\displaystyle A:=\begin{pmatrix}wI_{d}&I_{d}\\
zI_{d}&I_{d}\end{pmatrix},

where
w\in[0,\frac{1}{2}\gamma)
,
z=\gamma-w
. We will define the errors with and without this transformation,

\displaystyle e_{n}

\displaystyle:=\big\|Q_{n}-q_{t_{n}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{e}_{n}

\displaystyle:=\big\|A(Q_{n}-q_{t_{n}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}.

Let
\sigma_{\max}=\|A\|_{2}
and
\sigma_{\min}
denote the smallest and largest singular values of
A
respectively. Since
A
is invertible, the singular value
\sigma_{\min}
is non-zero and will satisfy
\sigma_{\min}=\|A^{-1}\|_{2}^{-1}
. Let
\kappa=\frac{\sigma_{\max}}{\sigma_{\min}}
denote the condition number of
A
. We can use
\widetilde{e}_{n}
to bound the error
e_{n}
as

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\hskip 0.7113pte_{n}\leq\widetilde{e}_{n}\leq\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pte_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt.

We now consider a third process
\widehat{Q}=(\widehat{X},\widehat{V})^{\top}
where
(\widehat{X}_{n+1}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widehat{V}_{n+1})
is obtained by applying the QUICSORT method (
1
) to the ULD diffusion at time
t_{n}
with the same underlying Brownian motion as both
Q
and
q
. This allows us to estimate the local error between the coordinate-changed approximation
AQ_{n+1}
and diffusion process
Aq_{t_{n+1}}
at time
t_{n+1}
in terms of the same error at time
t_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
. By Theorem
10
, we can bound the local strong error as

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{Q}_{n}-q_{t_{n}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq C_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}\hskip 0.7113pth^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{s}},

and the local weak error as

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[\widehat{Q}_{n}-q_{t_{n}}\big]\big\|_{2}\leq C_{w}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}\hskip 0.7113pth^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{w}},

where
C_{s}\hskip 0.7113pt,C_{w}\hskip 0.7113pt,q,p_{s}\hskip 0.7113pt,p_{w}>0
are the following constants (which do not depend on
d
and
h
).

Assumptions

C_{s}

C_{w}

q

p_{s}

p_{w}

(
A.1
), (
A.2
)

C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{s,x}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{s,v}

C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,x}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(1)}_{w,v}

0.5

(
A.1
), (

), (

)

C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{s,x}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{s,v}

C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,x}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(2)}_{w,v}

1

(
A.1
), (

), (

), (
A.4
)

C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,x}+C^{\hskip 0.7113pt(3)}_{w,v}

1.5

3.5

4

In the above table, we also note that
p_{s}+\frac{1}{2}\geq p_{w}\hskip 0.7113pt
. We now define the following variables

\displaystyle y_{n}

\displaystyle:=A\big(Q_{n}-q_{t_{n}}\big)=\begin{pmatrix}(wX_{n}+V_{n})-(wx_{t_{n}}+v_{t_{n}})\\[3.0pt]
(zX_{n}+V_{n})-(zx_{t_{n}}+v_{t_{n}})\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\widetilde{y}_{n}

\displaystyle:=A\big(Q_{n}-\widehat{Q}_{n}\big)=\begin{pmatrix}(wX_{n}+V_{n})-(w\widehat{X}_{n}+\widehat{V}_{n})\\[3.0pt]
(zX_{n}+V_{n})-(z\widehat{X}_{n}+\widehat{V}_{n})\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\widehat{y}_{n}

\displaystyle:=A\big(\widehat{Q}_{n}-q_{t_{n}}\big)=\begin{pmatrix}(w\widehat{X}_{n}+\widehat{V}_{n})-(wx_{t_{n}}+v_{t_{n}})\\[3.0pt]
(z\widehat{X}_{n}+\widehat{V}_{n})-(zx_{t_{n}}+v_{t_{n}})\end{pmatrix},

and note that
y_{n}=\widetilde{y}_{n}+\widehat{y}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
. Thus, the coordinate-transformed error
\widetilde{e}_{n}
can be estimated as

\displaystyle\widetilde{e}_{n+1}

\displaystyle=\|y_{n+1}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}=\big\|\widetilde{y}_{n+1}+\widehat{y}_{n+1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq\big\|\widetilde{y}_{n+1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|\widehat{y}_{n+1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\hskip 0.7113pt.

We bound the first term using the contractivity of the QUICSORT method (Theorem
28
),

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{y}_{n+1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq\sqrt{1-2\alpha h}\,\widetilde{e}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,

and the second term can be bounded using the local strong error,

\displaystyle\big\|\hskip 0.7113pt\widehat{y}_{n+1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}}.

Therefore, we obtain the following estimate of the coordinate-transformed error,

\displaystyle\widetilde{e}_{n+1}\leq\sqrt{1-2\alpha h}\,\widetilde{e}_{n}+\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}}.

(87)

By setting
w=0
and using Remark
29
, we have
\alpha>0
provided
h
is sufficiently small.
Similarly, by assuming
h
is sufficiently small so that
\alpha h\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we obtain the below inequality.

\displaystyle\sqrt{1-2\alpha h}\leq\sqrt{1-2\alpha h+\alpha^{2}h^{2}}=1-\alpha h.

Therefore, using this inequality and by applying the inequality (
87
)
n
times to
\widetilde{e}_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we obtain

\displaystyle\widetilde{e}_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\sqrt{1-2\alpha h}\,\big)^{n}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\big(\sqrt{1-2\alpha h}\,\big)^{k}\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\alpha h\big)^{n}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{0}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\big(1-\alpha h\big)^{k}\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}}

\displaystyle=\big(1-\alpha h\big)^{n}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{0}+\bigg(\frac{1-(1-\alpha h)^{n}}{1-(1-\alpha h)}\bigg)\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\alpha h\big)^{n}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{0}+\bigg(\frac{1}{1-(1-\alpha h)}\bigg)\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}}

\displaystyle\leq e^{-n\alpha h}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{0}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}-1}.

Since
e_{n}\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}
and
\widetilde{e}_{0}\leq\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pte_{0}\hskip 0.7113pt
, it follows that the error
e_{n}
can be bounded as

\displaystyle e_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{A}e^{-n\alpha h}e_{0}+\kappa_{A}\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha}C_{s}\bigg)d^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{p_{s}-1},

(88)

for
n\geq 0
. Using
e_{0}=\|X_{0}-x_{0}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}
and
\|X_{n}-x_{t_{n}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq e_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
, the inequality (
88
) gives the first two bounds (under the assumptions ((
A.1
), (
A.2
)) and ((
A.1
), (
A.2
), (
A.3
)) respectively).
However, the estimate would only be
O(h^{2.5})
in the third case. Thus, we now expand
\widetilde{e}_{n}
as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\widetilde{e}_{n+1}^{\,2}&=\big\|\widetilde{y}_{n+1}+\widehat{y}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\\[3.0pt]
&=\big\|\widetilde{y}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|\widehat{y}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+2\hskip 0.7113pt\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{y}_{n+1},\widehat{y}_{n+1}\rangle\big].\end{split}

We bound the first term using the contractivity of the QUICSORT method (Theorem
28
),

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{y}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq(1-2\alpha h)\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}^{\,2}\hskip 0.7113pt,

and the second term can be bounded using the local strong error,

\displaystyle\big\|\hskip 0.7113pt\widehat{y}_{n+1}\big\|^{2}_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC^{2}_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{\hskip 0.7113pt2q}h^{2p_{s}}.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{n}
be the filtration underlying the processes at time
t_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
. Then, by the tower property of conditional expectation and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we can bound the third term

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{y}_{n+1},\widehat{y}_{n+1}\rangle\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\hskip 0.7113pty_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widehat{y}_{n+1}\rangle\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{y}_{n+1}-y_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widehat{y}_{n+1}\rangle\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big[\big\langle y_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\mathbb{E}\big[\hskip 0.7113pt\widehat{y}_{n+1}|\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\rangle\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{y}_{n+1}-y_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt,\widehat{y}_{n+1}\rangle\big]

\displaystyle\leq\big\|y_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big\|\hskip 0.7113pt\mathbb{E}\big[\hskip 0.7113pt\widehat{y}_{n+1}|\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}+\big\|\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{y}_{n+1}-y_{n}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big\|\hskip 0.7113pt\widehat{y}_{n+1}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{e}_{n}\|A\|_{2}\big\|\mathbb{E}\big[\hskip 0.7113pt\widehat{Q}_{n+1}-q_{t_{n+1}}|\mathcal{F}_{n}\big]\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\|A\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\big(Q_{n+1}-\widehat{Q}_{n+1}\big)-(Q_{n}-q_{t_{n}})\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\big\|\widehat{Q}_{n+1}-q_{t_{n+1}}\big\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}.

Using the local strong error bounds for QUICSORT, Theorem
38
and Corollary
36
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{y}_{n+1},\widehat{y}_{n+1}\rangle\big]

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}C_{w}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{q}\hskip 0.7113pth^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{w}}+\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}e_{n}h\cdot C_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{q}h^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{s}}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)d^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{w}},

where we also used the assumption that
h\leq 1
. Putting everything together and applying the inequality (
43
) in Theorem
24
with
c=\frac{1}{\alpha h}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we obtain

\displaystyle\widetilde{e}_{n+1}^{\,2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-2\alpha h\big)\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}^{\,2}+\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC^{2}_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{2q}h^{2p_{s}}+2\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)d^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{w}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-2\alpha h\big)\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}^{\,2}+\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113ptC^{2}_{s}\hskip 0.7113ptd^{2q}h^{2p_{s}}+\alpha h\widetilde{e}_{n}^{\,2}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\sigma^{2}_{\max}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)^{2}d^{\hskip 0.7113pt2q}h^{\hskip 0.7113pt2p_{w}-1}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\alpha h\big)\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}^{\,2}+\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big(C^{2}_{s}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)^{2}\Big)d^{\hskip 0.7113pt2q}h^{\hskip 0.7113pt2p_{w}-1}.

Applying the above inequality
n
times to
\widetilde{e}_{n}
gives

\displaystyle\widetilde{e}_{n}^{\,2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1-\alpha h\big)^{n}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{0}^{\,2}+\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big(C^{2}_{s}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)^{2}\Big)d^{\hskip 0.7113pt2q}h^{\hskip 0.7113pt2p_{w}-1}\sum^{n-1}_{i=0}\big(1-\alpha h\big)^{i}

\displaystyle=\big(1-\alpha h\big)^{n}\widetilde{e}_{0}^{\,2}+\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pt\Big(C^{2}_{s}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)^{2}\Big)d^{\hskip 0.7113pt2q}h^{\hskip 0.7113pt2p_{w}-1}\bigg(\frac{1-(1-\alpha h)^{n}}{\alpha h}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq e^{-n\alpha h}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{0}^{\,2}+\sigma^{2}_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pt\bigg(\frac{1}{\alpha}C^{2}_{s}+\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)^{2}\bigg)d^{\hskip 0.7113pt2q}h^{\hskip 0.7113pt2p_{w}-2}.

Like earlier, we set
w=0
and used Remark
29
so that
0<\alpha h\leq 1
if
h
is sufficiently small.
Taking a square root on both sides of the inequality and using
\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}}\leq a+b
for
a,b\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\widetilde{e}_{n}\leq e^{-\frac{1}{2}n\alpha h}\widetilde{e}_{0}+\sigma_{\max}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)\bigg)d^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{w}-1}.

Using
e_{n}\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}}\hskip 0.7113pt\widetilde{e}_{n}
and
\widetilde{e}_{0}\leq\sigma_{\max}\hskip 0.7113pte_{0}\hskip 0.7113pt
, we obtain

\displaystyle e_{n}\leq\kappa_{A}e^{-\frac{1}{2}n\alpha h}e_{0}+\kappa_{A}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\alpha}}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\big(C_{w}+\kappa_{A}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{q}\hskip 0.7113ptC_{s}\big)\bigg)d^{\hskip 0.7113ptq}h^{\hskip 0.7113ptp_{w}-1},

for all
n\geq 0
. The third result now follows since
e_{0}=\|X_{0}-x_{0}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}
and
\|X_{n}-x_{t_{n}}\|_{\mathbb{L}_{2}}\leq e_{n}\hskip 0.7113pt
.
∎
